The Great Filter hypothesis posits one or more extraordinarily improbable evolutionary barriers between prebiotic chemistry and durable interstellar expansion, interpreting cosmic silence as an objective civilizational sieve; it reaches its boundary when inverting the silence into a diagnosis of collective human complacency, because a civilization possesses no unified decision-making agency and macroscopic outcomes are mere statistical aggregates of discrete individual tradeoffs; it remains useful for categorizing macroscopic technological milestones and transitional probabilities.

→ [费米悖论、主体性错配与大过滤器的投射 / The Fermi Paradox, Reified Agency, and the Projection of the Great Filter](../../posts/the-fermi-paradox-reified-agency-and-the-great-filter/)

### 行为经济学 / Behavioral Economics
`经济 / Economic`

行为经济学把损失厌恶、概率加权等偏离与环境作为修正加进理性选择装置，在让对既往选择的重构更细致时有用；它在此到了边界：先验被修正而从未被挑战，共享的形式装置为选择奠基这一核心前提被保留下来，可携带的演算仍被当作对能动的描述。

Behavioral economics adds departures such as loss aversion and probability weighting, and environments, as revisions to the rational-choice apparatus, which is of use in densifying the reconstruction of past choices; it reaches its boundary in that the prior is conditioned and never challenged, keeping the core premise that a shared formal apparatus grounds choice, so the portable calculus still stands as the description of agency.

→ [理性选择公设是原罪 / The Rational Choice Postulate Is the Original Sin](../../posts/the-rational-choice-postulate-is-the-original-sin/) · [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 生物基因决定论 / Biological Determinism
`科学 / Scientific`

把人的行为归于基因、激素与神经回路，在描述选择所受的身体约束时有用；它在此到了边界：生物学的测量是观察者地平线内的相关与约束，不是选择的发起者，约束界定的是选项的菜单，不是选择者，把青少年的决定归于大脑发育，替父母开脱，也冻结了年轻人自己的学习回路。

Attributing human behaviour to genes, hormones and neural circuitry is of use in describing the bodily constraints on choice; it reaches its boundary in that biological measurements are correlations and constraints within an observer's horizon, not the originator of the choice, constraints define the menu of options rather than the chooser, and crediting adolescents' decisions to brain development excuses parents while freezing young people's own learning loop.

→ [引导不是占有 / Guidance Is Not Ownership](../../posts/guidance-is-not-ownership/)

### 比特币作为价值储存 / Bitcoin as Store of Value
`经济 / Economic`

比特币起初是一件让人在法币体系之外表达能动性的开放媒介，作为交换的工具有用；把价值归给代币本身、把它当作静态的价值储存，在此到了边界，媒介冻结了资本，转成零和的投机，又靠法币支撑的稳定币补上交易用途。

Bitcoin began as an open medium for expressing agency outside the fiat system, of use as an instrument of exchange; crediting value to the token itself and treating it as a static store of value is where it reaches its boundary, as the medium freezes capital, turns into zero-sum speculation, and leans on fiat-backed stablecoins to patch its transactional use.

→ [自我价值、反向财富转移与过去成功的陷阱 / Self-Worthiness, Reverse Wealth Transfer, and the Trap of Past Success](../../posts/self-worthiness-reverse-wealth-transfer-and-the-trap-of-past-success/)

### 块状宇宙世界线理论 / Block Universe Worldline
`科学 / Scientific`

块状宇宙诠释将相对论中随参照系而异的同时性外推为过去、现在与未来平等并存的永恒四维流形，在跨参照系协调时空轨迹时有用；它错把在当下绘出的静态几何地图当成了活生生的因果展开，在此到达了边界。

The block-universe interpretation extrapolates frame-relative simultaneity in relativity into an eternal four-dimensional manifold where past, present, and future coexist equally, which is of use for coordinating spacetime trajectories across reference frames; it reaches its boundary in mistaking a static geometric map drawn in the present for lived causal unfolding.

→ [四维长鹿的幻象与当下的模型 / The Myth of the Four-Dimensional Deer and the Present Model](../../posts/the-myth-of-the-four-dimensional-deer-and-the-present-model/) · [心智的几何学 / The Geometry of Mind](../../posts/the-geometry-of-mind/) · [时间是因果的不可逆方向与离散步长 / Time Is the Irreversible Direction and Discrete Step of Causality](../../posts/time-is-causality-not-a-dimension/) · [黑白采样帧与外推宇宙 / The Black-and-White Sample Frame and the Extrapolated Universe](../../posts/the-black-and-white-sample-frame-and-the-extrapolated-universe/) · [索求万物理论是在将当下冻结为清单 / Demanding a Theory of Everything Freezes Time into a Catalog](../../posts/demanding-a-toe-freezes-time-into-a-catalog/) · [时间之箭的伪悖论与对称模拟的边界 / The Mirage of Time's Arrow and the Boundaries of Symmetric Simulation](../../posts/the-mirage-of-times-arrow-and-the-boundaries-of-simulation/) · [工具的幻觉与时间的倒置 / The Illusion of the Tool and the Inversion of Time](../../posts/the-illusion-of-the-tool-and-the-inversion-of-time/)

### 混沌理论 / Chaos Theory
`科学 / Scientific`

混沌理论把敏感依赖与预测的界限形式化，在命名确定性系统里长期预测的界限时有用；它在此到了边界：命名并数学化这份开放的动作，又重新装上一幅一致的图景，未知在其中显得已被标绘、可以理解，而极端的打开仍从任何被形式化的混沌之外到来。

Chaos theory formalizes sensitive dependence and the limits of prediction, which is of use in naming the limits of long-range prediction in deterministic systems; it reaches its boundary in that the act of naming and mathematizing that openness reinstalls a picture of coherence in which the unknown appears charted and intelligible, while extreme openings keep arriving from outside any formalized chaos.

→ [现实扭曲力场颠倒了基线 / The Reality Distortion Field Inverts the Baseline](../../posts/the-reality-distortion-field-inverts-the-baseline/) · [递归形式系统中作为记忆与控制的边界条件图 / Boundary-Condition Graphs as Memory and Control in Recursive Formal Systems](../../posts/boundary-condition-graphs-as-memory-and-control-in-recursive-formal-systems/) · [没有固定者的固定 / Fixed by Nothing](../../posts/fixed-by-nothing/)

### 丘奇-图灵论题 / Church-Turing Thesis
`数学 / Mathematical`

丘奇-图灵论题将有效可计算函数等同于图灵机在离散符号串上可计算的函数，奠定了计算机科学的基础不变类；一旦在预编码符号之上的形式可计算性被等同于穷尽的实在或活态认知，遗漏了选定编码并重拟问题的那道未形式化的第一人称切分，该论题便到达了边界。

The Church-Turing thesis identifies effectively calculable functions with those computable by a Turing machine over discrete symbol strings, which provides the foundational invariance class of computer science; it reaches its boundary when formal computability over pre-encoded symbols is equated with exhaustive reality or living cognition, omitting the unformalized first-person cut that chooses the encoding and re-frames the problem.

→ [令人彻夜难眠的四个追问与投射的边界 / Four Questions That Keep You Up at Night](../../posts/four-night-questions/) · [保真度而非最优化 / Fidelity, Not Optimality](../../posts/fidelity-not-optimality/) · [底层表面 / The Surface Beneath](../../posts/the-surface-beneath/)

### 古典功利主义 / Classical Utilitarianism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

最大幸福原则把幸福当作可以跨人加总的量，在比较政策的总体后果时有用；它在此到了边界：波普尔的修正保留了同一个外部供给的前提，只是调换了计入的一方。

The greatest happiness principle treats happiness as a quantity addable across persons, which is of use in comparing the overall consequences of policies; it reaches its boundary in that Popper’s correction keeps the same premise of external supply and only reverses which side counts.

→ [最初的区分 / The Initial Distinction](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 集体无意识 / Collective Unconscious
`哲学 / Philosophical`

集体无意识预设一种先于个人的深层结构，在描述许多人共有的倾向时有用；它在此到了边界：追溯下去，它是交出更新与代为决定相互对接、在许多心智间稳定之后留下的共振痕迹，机制落在每个心智是否把误差传回自己可更新的参数。

The collective unconscious presumes a deep structure prior to individuals, which is of use in describing tendencies many people share; it reaches its boundary in that, traced back, it is the resonance left when handing over updates meets complementary deciding-for-others and stabilizes across many minds, while the mechanism rests on whether each mind carries the error back to its own updatable parameters.

→ [人生是一个持续的自我迭代过程 / Life as a Continuous Process of Self-Iteration](../../posts/ren-sheng-shi-yi-ge-chi-xu-de-zi-wo-die-dai-guo-cheng/)

### 无理解的能力 / Competence Without Comprehension
`哲学 / Philosophical`

“无理解的能力”论题主张复杂功能执行可以在没有意识理解的情况下运作，在分析演化适应与自动化机制时有用；一旦流畅的输出词元被误认作具有体验的心智，或被用来将有意识的理解视作可有可无之物，便到达了边界。

The competence-without-comprehension thesis holds that complex functional execution can operate without conscious comprehension, which is of use in analyzing evolutionary adaptation and automated mechanisms; it reaches its boundary when fluent output tokens are mistaken for an experiencing mind or used to treat conscious understanding as dispensable.

→ [大倒置的消解 / The Dissolution of the Great Reversal](../../posts/the-dissolution-of-the-great-reversal/)

### 宪政人工智能 / Constitutional AI
`技术 / Technological`

宪政AI预设伦理可通过声明性原则工程化；对安全防护有效，却把心智的主权道德决断外化为静态条文。

Constitutional AI presumes ethics can be operationalized via declarative principles; effective for safety guardrails, it externalizes sovereign moral judgment into static text.

→ [名为 Claude 的投射 / The Projection Called Claude](../../posts/claudes-constitution-analysis/) · [良知、委托与管辖权错位 / Conscience, Delegation, and the Jurisdiction Error](../../posts/conscience-power-delegation/)

### 批判理性主义 / Critical Rationalism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

批判性排除能抛弃从某个区分推出的东西，在区分之内检验后果、排除不一致时有用；它在此到了边界：它消解不了使排除成为可能的那个区分，理性始于先于理性的一刀，那一刀是它的条件。

Critical elimination can discard what follows from a distinction, which is of use in testing consequences and removing inconsistencies inside that distinction; it reaches its boundary in that it cannot dissolve the distinction that made elimination possible, since rationality begins from a pre-rational cut that conditions it.

→ [最初的区分 / The Initial Distinction](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 达克效应 / Dunning–Kruger Effect
`科学 / Scientific`

达克效应预设自信与能力之间的落差可由一把共享尺度读出、低能者高估自身，在指出自我评估会偏离实际表现时有用；它在此到了边界：高能力同样会构筑更致密的内部合理化以隔绝自身前提免受修正，被当作自我觉察的阶梯援引时它使调用者免于触碰真实边界，而被施用于共识之外仍在推进的打开时则让与均值的一致充当了胜任的尺度。

The Dunning–Kruger effect presumes that the gap between confidence and competence can be read off a shared measure, with the low-skilled overestimating themselves, which is of use in noting that self-assessment can drift from performance; it reaches its boundary in that high capability also constructs denser internal rationalizations that insulate its own premises from correction, invoked as a ladder of self-awareness it exempts its user from contact with an actual boundary, and applied to an opening still under way outside the consensus it lets consistency with the average stand as the measure of competence.

→ [“知道自己不知道”是一种修辞假象 / Knowing That You Don't Know Is a Rhetorical Illusion](../../posts/zhi-dao-zi-ji-bu-zhi-dao-de-ren-zhi-jia-xiang/) · [现实扭曲力场颠倒了基线 / The Reality Distortion Field Inverts the Baseline](../../posts/the-reality-distortion-field-inverts-the-baseline/) · [智能最容易困住的正是它自己 / Intelligence Is the Thing Most Likely to Trap Itself](../../posts/intelligence-traps-itself/)

### 意识涌现论 / Emergence of Consciousness
`科学 / Scientific`

意识涌现论预设第一人称觉知与主体性会在粗粒化尺度上由无意识物质的复杂排列生成，这在组织神经与动力学相关物的第三人称模型时有用；它在此到了边界：对封闭确定性系统进行粗粒化只会改变描述长度，并不会生成新的独立变量，因此该理论无法从观察与抉择主体自身已划出的下游痕迹中反推出这一主体，且由于所谓大脑或神经网络本就是一次观察之内登记到的中间尺度共同同一性，要求它生成观察本身无异于要求被观察的后验走在观察着的先验之前。

The emergence of consciousness presumes that first-person awareness and agency arise at coarser scales from complex arrangements of non-conscious matter, which is of use in organizing third-person models of neural and dynamical correlates; it reaches its boundary in that coarse-graining a closed deterministic system alters only description length without generating a new independent variable, so the theory cannot derive the observing and choosing subject from downstream traces that subject has already drawn, and because what is called a brain or a neural network is already a mid-scale collective identity registered within an observation, asking it to generate the observing asks the observed posterior to precede the observing prior.

→ [主体性不曾涌现 / Agency Does Not Arise](../../posts/agency-does-not-arise/) · [抽象、决定，与主流机器学习的三大结构性症结 / Abstraction, Determination, and the Three Structural Flaws of Machine Learning](../../posts/abstraction-determination-and-the-three-structural-flaws-of-machine-learning/)

### 熵引力 / Entropic Gravity
`科学 / Scientific`

从熵或量子信息推导引力，把质量读作集中到足以登记为物质的信息，把引力读作这种集中的统计趋势，在复现熟悉的极限、重新诠释引力的微弱时有用；它在此到了边界：它仍是一次选择，舍弃了一份无穷的细节余量，它的临时封闭性可用，却不免于重新诠释。

Deriving gravity from entropy or quantum information reads mass as information concentrated enough to register as matter and gravity as a statistical tendency of that concentration, which is of use in recovering familiar limits and reinterpreting gravity's weakness; it reaches its boundary in that it remains a selection that discards an unbounded remainder of detail, its temporary closure usable but not exempt from reinterpretation.

→ [选择、矛盾与开放的现实 / Selection, Contradiction, and Open Reality](../../posts/selection-contradiction-and-open-reality/)

### 期望效用理论 / Expected Utility Theory
`经济 / Economic`

期望效用理论把一套一致的偏好结构当作可以在人与人之间搬运，在一场具体的交换里整理选择时有用；它在此到了边界：每个心智都在本地重新诠释它，它只在特定的交换里好用，而不是一条可以转移的论断。

Expected utility theory treats one coherent preference structure as interpersonally portable, which is of use in organizing choices inside a particular exchange; it reaches its boundary in that each mind reinterprets it locally, so it serves inside particular exchanges rather than as a transferable thesis.

→ [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 延展心智理论 / Extended Mind Thesis
`哲学 / Philosophical`

延展心智理论主张外部工具构成心智的一部分；适用于分析计算支架，却模糊了第一人称觉知的主体界限。

Extended cognition argues external artifacts form part of the mind; useful for analyzing computational scaffolds, it blurs the boundary of conscious first-person awareness.

→ [作为认知主动延伸的工具 / Tools as Active Extensions of Cognition](../../posts/extended-cognition-refined/) · [维度即投影 / Dimensions Are Projections](../../posts/dimensions-are-projections/) · [递归形式系统中作为记忆与控制的边界条件图 / Boundary-Condition Graphs as Memory and Control in Recursive Formal Systems](../../posts/boundary-condition-graphs-as-memory-and-control-in-recursive-formal-systems/)

### 证伪主义 / Falsificationism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

证伪主义主张理论只能被反驳、不能被最终证实，在把判断放到能被推翻的位置上时有用；它在此到了边界：每次检验都晚于它所针对的过程一步，方法本身因此可修正，无论是借解释掉反面证据来规避证伪，还是借驳倒对方来证明自己而误用证伪，都会把它所针对的开放过程当成已结的案子合上。

Falsificationism holds that theories can be refuted but never finally confirmed, which is of use in placing a judgment where it can be overturned; it reaches its boundary in that each test lags the process it targets, so the method itself stays revisable, and whether it is evaded by explaining away counterevidence or misused by refuting opponents as self-proof, the open process it addresses is closed into a settled case.

→ [关于归因 / On Attribution](../../posts/guan-yu-gui-yin/) · [从形式迷思到逻辑闭环 / From the Myth of Form to the Closed Loop of Logic](../../posts/cong-xing-shi-mi-si-dao-luo-ji-bi-huan/) · [能动性与模式 / Agency and the Pattern](../../posts/agency-and-the-pattern/)

### 费米悖论 / The Fermi Paradox
`科学 / Scientific`

费米悖论预设地外智能会在与人相近的时间窗口里、以按人的感官格式化的信号出现，于是追问它们为何沉默；这个预设在窗口之外到了边界，因为一个真正“外”的回路无法被认出，一旦能握手便不再是“外”，作为给搜寻一个可操作对象的问题，它依然有用。

The Fermi paradox presumes extraterrestrial intelligence would appear in a time window close to the human one, with signals formatted to human senses, and so asks why it is silent; the presumption reaches its boundary outside that window, since a truly other loop cannot be recognized and stops being other once a handshake is made, while as a question that gives the search something to operate on it stays useful.

→ [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/) · [费米悖论、主体性错配与大过滤器的投射 / The Fermi Paradox, Reified Agency, and the Projection of the Great Filter](../../posts/the-fermi-paradox-reified-agency-and-the-great-filter/)

### 有限游戏与无限游戏 / Finite and Infinite Games
`哲学 / Philosophical`

詹姆斯·卡斯把为赢而玩、规则既定的有限游戏，与为让游戏继续而玩、边界可以扩展的无限游戏分开；这一区分在被当成两类人的标签时到了边界，因为同一个心智在不同时刻两种都在玩，作为看清地位竞赛只是一场有限游戏的工具，它依然锋利。

James Carse set finite games, played to win within fixed rules, apart from the infinite game, played to keep the play going with an extendable boundary; the distinction reaches its boundary when it is used to label two kinds of people, since the same Mind plays both at different moments, while as a tool for seeing that a status contest is only a finite game it stays sharp.

→ [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/) · [自我价值、反向财富转移与过去成功的陷阱 / Self-Worthiness, Reverse Wealth Transfer, and the Trap of Past Success](../../posts/self-worthiness-reverse-wealth-transfer-and-the-trap-of-past-success/) · [成功的几何学 / The Geometry of Success](../../posts/the-geometry-of-success/)

### 财政乘数 / Fiscal Multiplier
`经济 / Economic`

财政乘数假定一笔公共支出经由接续几轮花费能按自身倍数抬升总收入，在追踪支出如何在经济体中传导时有用；它在此到了边界：这笔支出事先取自私人之手，“投资”标签把这次重新调配记作供私人中心倍增的新存量，而倍增一旦发生，依然归属于承担上行与下行后果的各个私人中心，不属于任何集体所在。

The fiscal multiplier presumes that a public outlay, through successive rounds of spending, raises income by a multiple of itself, which is of use in tracing how spending passes through an economy; it reaches its boundary in that the outlay was first removed from private hands, the investment label scores that reallocation as a new stock for private centers to multiply, and multiplication, where it occurs, still belongs to the private centers that bear upside and downside, not to a collective locus.

→ [公共支出不创造资本 / Public Spending Does Not Create Capital](../../posts/public-spending-does-not-create-capital/)

### 地平说认识论 / Flat Earth Epistemology
`科学 / Scientific`

地平说认识论把日常行走所处的局部平坦切平面当作世界的全局几何，在曲率可忽略不计的脚下近处步幅内依然准确高效；一旦跨越曲率不断累积的行星视界，它便到达了边界——恰如不经局部切平面重校准便强套全局球面同样会在脚下踩空。

Flat-earth epistemology treats the locally flat tangent plane of everyday walking as the global geometry of the world, which remains accurate and efficient for immediate local stride where curvature is negligible; it reaches its boundary across planetary horizons where curvature accumulates, just as imposing a global sphere without local tangent recalibration stumbles over immediate ground.

→ [路口的转向与地平说的行动悖论 / Turning at the Crossroad and the Flat-Earth Action Paradox](../../posts/lu-kou-de-zhuan-xiang-yu-di-ping-shuo-de-xing-dong-bei-lun/) · [切平面的行者与完美球面的踩空者 / The Walking Tangent and the Stumbling Sphere](../../posts/the-walking-tangent-and-the-stumbling-sphere/) · [投影的阶梯与因果的锚点 / The Ladder of Projections and the Anchor of Causality](../../posts/tou-ying-de-jie-ti-yu-yin-guo-de-mao-dian/)

### 功能性社会层级论 / Functional Social Hierarchy
`哲学 / Philosophical`

功能性的层级论预设，位次是能力差异遇上共同任务时沉淀下来的，它在解释差异如何在一种共同要求之下排成位置时有用；它在此到了边界：秩序属于许多人共同持有的那把尺，尺一换，秩序就换，它既不是从外部加在差异上的结构，也不是从外部落到一个较低位置上的原因。

Functional hierarchy presumes that rank precipitates from differences in capacity meeting shared tasks, which is of use in explaining how difference orders into places under a common demand; it reaches its boundary in that the order belongs to the measure many hold in common and changes when the measure does, and it is neither a structure laid on difference from outside nor a cause arriving at a lower place from outside.

→ [来自个体差异的层级 / Hierarchy from Individual Difference](../../posts/hierarchy-from-individual-difference/)

### 心灵哲学功能主义 / Functionalism (Philosophy of Mind)
`哲学 / Philosophical`

功能主义以因果角色界定心理状态，其前提是描述者可以立于被描述的心智之外，这一前提在理论被扩展到持有它的心智自身时失效，因为认真持有与还原主观性都必须占据它所抹去的观察者之位；作为描述信息处理多重实现、指导系统设计与比较的工具，它依然锋利有用。

Functionalism defines mental states by causal role on the premise that the describer can stand outside the mind described; the premise fails once the theory is extended to the mind holding it, since both serious endorsement and the reduction of subjectivity must occupy the observer position it erases, while as a tool for describing multiply realizable information processing and guiding system design it remains sharp and useful.

→ [功能主义者无法持有的功能主义 / The Functionalism No Functionalist Can Hold](../../posts/the-functionalism-no-functionalist-can-hold/) · [无法闭合的痕迹 / The Unclosable Trace](../../posts/the-unclosable-trace/) · [飞行的类比碰不到心智 / The Flight Analogy Leaves the Mind Untouched](../../posts/the-flight-analogy-leaves-the-mind-untouched/) · [集聚与人造主体性 / Collective and Artificial Subjectivity](../../posts/subtracting-consciousness-at-the-source/) · [因果的自反性与物理的无源假定 / Causality is Irreducible; The Physical is a View from Nowhere](../../posts/causality-is-irreducible-the-physical-is-a-view-from-nowhere/)

### AGI功能主义理论 / Functionalist Theory of AGI
`技术 / Technological`

AGI的功能主义参照预设，智能就是对抽象的占有与操作，因而足够的规模、检索与重组即构成通用智能；在度量并改进存储、检索与重组抽象产物的工具时，它依然有用，而当由系统被优化去满足的同一套标准所界定的证据被要求审视这一前提，当抽象的产物被当作划出并修订它们的那种能力时，它便到了边界。

The functionalist reference for AGI presumes that intelligence is the possession and manipulation of abstractions, so that enough scale, retrieval, and recombination amount to general intelligence; it stays useful for measuring and improving tools that store, retrieve, and recombine the products of abstraction, and reaches its boundary where evidence defined by the criteria the systems are optimized to satisfy is asked to examine that premise, and where the products of abstraction are taken for the capacity that draws and revises them.

→ [AGI的预设与来自外部的视角 / The Presumption of AGI and the View from Outside](../../posts/the-presumption-of-agi-and-the-view-from-outside/) · [观察中的智能与残余间隙 / Intelligence in Observation and the Residual Gap](../../posts/intelligence-in-observation-and-the-residual-gap/)

### 博弈论 / Game Theory
`数学 / Mathematical`

均衡分析假定各个主体关于价值与风险的内部地图可以互换，在模型之内推出严密的结论时有用；它在此到了边界：它的建议会遇到按自己的前提重新诠释它的心智。

Equilibrium analysis assumes agents' internal maps of value and risk are interchangeable, which is of use in drawing rigorous conclusions inside the model; it reaches its boundary in that its prescriptions meet minds that re-render them under their own premises.

→ [理性从不跨越心智 / Rationality Never Travels Across the Mind](../../posts/rationality-never-travels-across-the-mind/)

### 广义相对论 / General Relativity
`科学 / Scientific`

广义相对论将引力建模为与应力-能量相耦合的光滑四维时空流形的局部弯曲，在行星动力学、引力透镜与相对论时钟校准中给出了高精度预测；当光滑流形的外推撞上奇点，或被读作脱离观察者的已完成块状宇宙而非校准于有限观测分辨率的连续框架时，便到达了边界。

General relativity models gravitation as the local curvature of a smooth four-dimensional spacetime manifold coupled to stress-energy, which yields high-precision predictions across planetary dynamics, gravitational lensing, and relativistic clocks; it reaches its boundary when smooth manifold extrapolation hits singularities or is read as a completed observer-independent block cosmos rather than a continuous frame calibrated to finite observational resolution.

→ [框架的边界 / The Boundary of the Frame](../../posts/the-boundary-of-the-frame/) · [全息回声 / The Holographic Echo](../../posts/the-holographic-echo-reverse-engineering-quantum-indeterminacy-via-the-riemann-zeta-function/)

### 丰裕时代的生成性价值 / Generative Values of Abundance
`经济 / Economic`

数字经济学指出免费复制品使价值迁往即时性、个性化与可寻性等不可复制的品质，在测绘后稀缺商业模式时有用；一旦这八项分类被当成封闭清单套用，而非对照移动中的用户瓶颈重新推导，便到达了边界。

Digital economics notes that free copies shift value toward uncopyable qualities such as immediacy, personalization, and findability, which is of use in mapping post-scarcity business models; it reaches its boundary once the eight-item taxonomy is treated as a closed checklist rather than re-derived against shifting user bottlenecks.

→ [清单合上之后，胜过免费 / Better Than Free After the Checklist Closes](../../posts/better-than-free-after-the-checklist-closes/)

### 梯度下降法 / Gradient Descent
`技术 / Technological`

梯度下降法沿着可微损失曲面上的局部一阶导数步进，在选定架构与目标函数内部优化参数时行之有效；一旦被当作自主寻真的智能体看待便到达了边界，因为局部斜率不带任何全局远见、仅捕获固定的一层，而盆地选择、架构设计与目标切换皆在梯度之外。

Gradient descent steps along local first-order derivatives across a differentiable loss surface, which is effective for optimizing parameters inside a chosen architecture and objective; it reaches its boundary when treated as an autonomous truth-seeking agent, since local slopes carry no global foresight and capture only one fixed layer while basin selection, architecture, and objective shifts sit outside the gradient.

→ [不知去向何方的梯度下降 / The Descent That Doesn't Know Where It's Going](../../posts/the-descent-that-doesnt-know-where-its-going/) · [模块化反馈回路与动态残差网络 / Modular Feedback Loops and Dynamic Residual Networks](../../posts/dynamic-resnet-of-skill-loops/) · [底层表面 / The Surface Beneath](../../posts/the-surface-beneath/)

### 哥德尔不完备定理 / Gödel's Incompleteness Theorems
`数学 / Mathematical`

哥德尔证明任何一致、可有效公理化且足以表达基本算术的形式系统都包含既无法在内部证明也无法证伪的独立命题，也无法在内部证明自身的一致性，这在精确标出形式系统的界限时有用；它在此到了边界：作为关于外部系统的命题，它可以被陈述、检验和传递，而陈述本身并不产生从区分活动内部对开放余项的登记；它被读成关于心智本身的证明时，同一个限制又在系统之外、在心智之间重现。

Gödel proves that any consistent, effectively axiomatized formal system strong enough to express basic arithmetic contains independent statements it can neither prove nor refute from within and cannot prove its own consistency, which is of use in stating the limits of formal systems precisely; it reaches its boundary in that, as a proposition about external systems, it can be stated, checked and transmitted while the stating itself does not produce registration of the open residue from within the distinguishing activity, and, when read as a proof about mind itself, the same limit reappears outside the system, between Minds.

→ [潜无限与数学思维的暂时闭合 / Potential Infinity and the Temporary Closures of Mathematical Thought](../../posts/potential-infinity-and-the-temporary-closures-of-mathematical-thought/) · [论封闭系统、开放心智与证明的界限 / On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [没有系统能被保持封闭 / No System Can Be Kept Closed](../../posts/no-system-can-be-kept-closed/) · [为何数学永远解不完 / Why Mathematics Can Never Be Solved](../../posts/why-mathematics-can-never-be-solved/) · [寻觅的语法与淡出的观察者 / The Grammar of Finding and the Fading Observer](../../posts/the-grammar-of-finding-and-the-fading-observer/) · [验证的倒置 / The Reversal of Verification](../../posts/the-reversal-of-verification/) · [递归形式系统中作为记忆与控制的边界条件图 / Boundary-Condition Graphs as Memory and Control in Recursive Formal Systems](../../posts/boundary-condition-graphs-as-memory-and-control-in-recursive-formal-systems/) · [进位无法在寄存器之内被命名 / The Carry That Cannot Be Named Inside the Register](../../posts/the-carry-that-cannot-be-named-inside-the-register/) · [两度显现的不完备性 / Incompleteness Exhibited Twice](../../posts/incompleteness-exhibited-twice/) · [论关于 P 与 NP 所能言说的边界 / On the Boundaries of What Can Be Said about P and NP](../../posts/on-the-boundaries-of-what-can-be-said-about-p-and-np/) · [残余与自由变量 / The Residue and the Free Variable](../../posts/the-residue-and-the-free-variable/)

### 成长型思维 / Growth Mindset
`成功学 / Self-Help`

成长型思维主张能力可以通过努力增长，而不是固定的禀赋；它在被当成事后给走在前面的人贴的标签、用来排名他人时到了边界，因为结果里还有落在身上的东西，作为不把当前位置当成守恒封闭盒子的取向，它依然好用。

The growth mindset holds that ability grows through effort rather than being a fixed endowment; it reaches its boundary when used as an after-the-fact label for those ahead and a way of ranking others, since outcomes also hold what fell to someone, while as an orientation that refuses to treat the present position as a conserved, closed box it stays useful.

→ [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/)

### 哈耶克价格机制 / Hayekian Price Mechanism
`经济 / Economic`

哈耶克价格机制预设，价格是在没有计划者的情况下协调分散知识的信号，它在解释相对价格如何塑造各中心在解读稀缺与回报时所发现的东西、并化为弥合缺口的激励时有用；它在此到了边界：传过去的是成交价，而不是作为存量从一个内在移到另一个内在的知识，每个中心都在自己的所在重新推导价格意味着什么，而成交只需要对同一物品相反的排序，不需要价值模型的同一。

The Hayekian price mechanism presumes the price to be a signal that coordinates dispersed knowledge without a planner, which is of use in explaining how a relative price patterns what each center finds when it reads scarcity and yield and so issues in incentives that close a shortfall; it reaches its boundary in that what travels is the print, not knowledge moving as a stock from one interior to another, each center re-derives at its own locus what the price means, and a clearing needs only opposite rankings of one good, not identity of models of value.

→ [价格是言说，理解是交易 / Price as Utterance, Understanding as Trade](../../posts/price-as-utterance-understanding-as-trade/)

### 希尔伯特数学大纲 / Hilbert's Program for Mathematics
`数学 / Mathematical`

希尔伯特数学大纲以一切数学命题皆可在完备、一致且可判定的形式系统中借助有穷方法得到裁定为前提，在确立形式化公理方法与元数学证明论时有用；它在此到了边界：任何一致、可有效公理化且足以表达基本算术的形式系统都包含在预期解释下成立却既无法在内部证明也无法证伪的命题，且无法从内部证明自身的一致性，将单次形式化持守当作穷尽地基的要求会在区分活动的推进中不断遭遇溢出。

Hilbert's program takes as its premise that all mathematical statements can be settled within a complete, consistent, and decidable formal system certified by finitary means, which is of use in establishing the formal axiomatic method and metamathematical proof theory; it reaches its boundary in that any consistent, effectively axiomatized system strong enough to express basic arithmetic contains sentences that hold in an intended interpretation yet can neither be proved nor refuted from within and cannot prove its own consistency from within, so that treating any single formal hold as exhaustive ground meets ongoing overflow as distinguishing continues.

→ [为何数学永远解不完 / Why Mathematics Can Never Be Solved](../../posts/why-mathematics-can-never-be-solved/) · [递归形式系统中作为记忆与控制的边界条件图 / Boundary-Condition Graphs as Memory and Control in Recursive Formal Systems](../../posts/boundary-condition-graphs-as-memory-and-control-in-recursive-formal-systems/) · [没有系统能被保持封闭 / No System Can Be Kept Closed](../../posts/no-system-can-be-kept-closed/)

### 内在形而上学 / Immanent Metaphysics
`哲学 / Philosophical`

内在形而上学以三个不可互换的模态为一切领域建模，以类与实例的循环把它们绑在一起，以不可通约定理表明对称与连续不能同时完美，这在拆解二元论与决定论时有用；把三分法则当作现实的地基，在此到了边界，循环是为了修补切分所割断的连续而造的机械。

Immanent metaphysics models every domain through three non-interchangeable modalities, binds them with a cycle of class and instance, and shows through the Incommensuration Theorem that symmetry and continuity cannot both be perfect, of use in taking apart dualism and determinism; taking the law of triplication as the bedrock of reality is where it reaches its boundary, since the cycle is machinery built to mend the continuity the cut severed.

→ [切分的三重影子 / The Three Shadows of the Cut](../../posts/the-three-shadows-of-the-cut/)

### 帝国崩溃演化律 / Imperial Collapse Dynamics
`意识形态 / Ideological`

把过度扩张、财政枯竭、制度衰败当作帝国崩溃的规律性原因，在为历史痕迹命名时有用；它在此到了边界：同样的标签解释不了各不相同的崩溃，帝国是许多中心的对齐被压缩到一个容量有限的位点上，崩溃来自自我形象被豁免了再校准。

Treating overstretch, fiscal exhaustion and institutional decay as lawlike causes of imperial collapse is of use in naming historical traces; it reaches its boundary in that the same labels cannot explain collapses that differ, since an empire is the alignment of many centres compressed onto a locus of finite capacity, and collapse comes from a self-image exempted from recalibration.

→ [战争、革命与帝国的衰落 / War, Revolution, and the Fall of Empires](../../posts/war-revolution-and-the-fall-of-empires/) · [维度即投影 / Dimensions Are Projections](../../posts/dimensions-are-projections/)

### 信息本体优先论 / Information as Ontologically Primary
`哲学 / Philosophical`

以信息为本、以物质为本，或视二者为双面，在严谨地发展一种描述、取得经验上的用处时有用；它在此到了边界：每一种立场都把自己的出发前提豁免于它施加给对手的审视，一种选择性的描述便呈现得仿佛并非选择，既有自洽的外观，也留下残余的不一致。

Taking information or matter as primary, or both as dual aspects, is of use in developing a description with rigor and empirical use; it reaches its boundary in that each position exempts its own starting premise from the scrutiny it applies to rivals, so a selective description presents itself as not selective, producing both the appearance of coherence and residual incoherence.

→ [选择、矛盾与开放的现实 / Selection, Contradiction, and Open Reality](../../posts/selection-contradiction-and-open-reality/) · [信息是什么 / What Information Is](../../posts/what-information-is/)

### 制度决定论 / Institutional Determinism
`经济 / Economic`

制度解释预设好制度造就能人，在描述约束、比较不同环境时有用；它在此到了边界：制度是先前自我选择稳定下来的残余，因果次序仍在做选择的个人一侧。

Institutional explanation presumes good institutions produce capable people, which is of use in describing constraints and comparing environments; it reaches its boundary in that institutions are the stabilized residue of prior self-selection, so the causal order stays with choosing individuals.

→ [平均值是残余，不是引擎 / The Average Is Residue, Not the Engine](../../posts/the-average-is-residue-not-the-engine/)

### 软件领域的杰文斯悖论 / Jevons Paradox in Software
`经济 / Economic`

杰文斯悖论指出效率提升降低单耗会引爆弹性需求；能解释市场规模爆发，却不描摹具体工种的质性重组。

Jevons paradox shows efficiency gains lower unit resource costs and trigger elastic demand; useful for market scaling, though blind to qualitative role redefinitions.

→ [AI 不会终结软件工程，而是将其扩展 / AI Won't Kill Software Engineering, It Will Expand It](../../posts/ai-wont-kill-software-engineering/)

### 柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度与所罗门诺夫归纳法 / Kolmogorov Complexity and Solomonoff Induction
`数学 / Mathematical`

柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度 K(f) 与所罗门诺夫归纳法通过最短程序描述长度以及横跨一切可计算机器的通用先验将简洁性与最优预测形式化，在算法信息论中作为理论参照上界时有用；它们的边界在于 K(f) 不可计算且通用搜索预设了一个预先枚举完毕的形式类，而鲜活的工程与探究则穿行于有界的行为保真度回路与无剧本的前提转换之中。

Kolmogorov complexity K(f) and Solomonoff induction formalize simplicity and optimal prediction through shortest-program description length and universal priors over all computable machines, which is of use as a theoretical reference bound in algorithmic information theory; they reach their boundary in that K(f) is uncomputable and universal search presupposes a pre-enumerated formal class, whereas living engineering and inquiry move through bounded behavioral fidelity loops and unscripted premise shifts.

→ [保真度而非最优化 / Fidelity, Not Optimality](../../posts/fidelity-not-optimality/) · [底层表面 / The Surface Beneath](../../posts/the-surface-beneath/) · [最小化路径 / The Minimizing Path](../../posts/minimizing-path/)

### 自由放任资本主义 / Laissez-faire Capitalism
`经济 / Economic`

自由放任资本主义把自愿生产与交易的秩序命名为一套经济制度，在描述一片不施强制的协作场域时有用；它在此到了边界：这个名称是事后的命名，不是引擎，不是这套制度产生自由的个人，而是认出自身能动、不发起强制的个人，产生了后来被冠以此名的样式。

Laissez-faire capitalism names the order of voluntary production and trade as an economic system, which is of use in describing a field of coordination free of initiated force; it reaches its boundary in that the name comes after the fact and is no engine, since the system does not produce free individuals but individuals who recognize their own agency and initiate no force produce the pattern later given that name.

→ [能动性的错配 / The Misallocation of Agency](../../posts/the-misallocation-of-agency/)

### 拉普拉斯决定论 / Laplacian Determinism
`科学 / Scientific`

拉普拉斯决定论假定穷尽掌握当下的位置、速度与固定定律即可决定未来一切状态，在支配规则可视为不变的轨道计算中依然好用；它在计算者自身未被纳入图景的位置——模型锁定正是将这一机制安进自身内部——以及将开放生成藏进无根基的初始条件这两处到达了边界。

Laplacian determinism presumes that complete knowledge of present positions, velocities, and fixed laws determines all future states, which stays useful for trajectory calculation where governing rules can be treated as unchanging; it reaches its boundary both at the calculator's own unincluded position—which model-lock installs inside oneself—and in hiding open generation inside ungrounded initial conditions.

→ [决定论机器 / The Deterministic Machine](../../posts/the-deterministic-machine/) · [没有固定者的固定 / Fixed by Nothing](../../posts/fixed-by-nothing/)

### 大数定律 / Law of Large Numbers
`数学 / Mathematical`

大数定律确立了随着独立试验不断累积，样本均值按 1/√N 的速率向系综期望收敛，在保险精算、热力学与宏观预测中颇具效用；一旦把聚合层面的平滑倒读为对单次试验的决定论号令，或借此将单一所在上的涨落斥为无因果效力的噪声，该定律便到达了边界。

The Law of Large Numbers establishes that sample averages converge toward ensemble expectations at rate 1/√N as independent trials accumulate, which is of use in insurance, thermodynamics, and macroscopic forecasting; it reaches its boundary when aggregate smoothing is read back as deterministic command over individual trials or used to dismiss single-locus variance as causally inert noise.

→ [确定性是微观不确定性的统计签名 / Determinism is the Statistical Signature of Micro-Indeterminism](../../posts/determinism-is-the-statistical-signature-of-micro-indeterminism/) · [阴影与无限 / The Shadow and the Infinite](../../posts/the-shadow-and-the-infinite/) · [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/) · [可见性的速度差 / The Velocity of Visibility](../../posts/the-velocity-of-visibility/) · [全息回声 / The Holographic Echo](../../posts/the-holographic-echo-reverse-engineering-quantum-indeterminacy-via-the-riemann-zeta-function/)

### 差异制造与因果解释决定论 / Difference-Making and Deterministic Explanation
`科学 / Scientific`

差异制造假定在定律与先前状态给定下，客观贡献可从反事实结果中读出；在封闭工程沙盘中有效，但输入与运作的边界必须先由外部视界给定，定律本身无法推导出自身被切分的前提。

Difference-making presumes that under given laws and prior conditions, causal contribution can be read off by counterfactual variation; effective within closed engineering models, its boundaries between input and operation must be stipulated from an external stance that the laws themselves cannot derive.

→ [信任读者是最后的越界 / Trusting the Reader Is the Last Overstep](../../posts/trusting-the-reader-is-the-last-overstep/)

### 解放叙事的俘虏身份陷阱 / Liberationist Identity Trap
`意识形态 / Ideological`

解放的言辞预设，观察者能从他人的行为里读出他们已经被俘，并由此成为他们自由的来源，在提醒人注意一个习惯或一种媒介的拉力时有用；它在此到了边界：把被观察到的动作定为他人之所是，就已经在这里画出了一份等级，而建立在这份定义之上的解放，只会让它更密，自我定义仍只能由那个继续着的所在来进行。

Liberation rhetoric presumes that an observer can read in another's behavior that they are already captive, and so become the source of their freedom, which is of use in drawing attention to the pull of a habit or a medium; it reaches its boundary in that defining the observed act as what the other is already draws a hierarchy here, a liberation built on that definition only densifies it, and self-definition can still be performed only by the locus that continues.

→ [解放的言辞始于定义囚禁 / Liberation Rhetoric Begins by Defining Captivity](../../posts/liberation-rhetoric-begins-by-defining-captivity/)

### 乘法过程产生的对数正态分布 / Log-normal Distribution from Multiplicative Processes
`数学 / Mathematical`

近似正态的对数增量逐步累加，使水平成为对数正态分布，这在描述复利下水平如何铺展时有用；它在此到了边界：它的右尾在有限尺度上常读作幂律，而正态与厚尾只是同一个递推的暂时视图，不是两条规律。

Summing approximately normal log increments makes the level log-normal, which is of use in describing how levels spread under compounding; it reaches its boundary in that its right tail frequently reads as a power law on finite scales, while the normal and the heavy tail are transient views of one recurrence, not two laws.

→ [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/)

### 逻辑斯蒂增长与S曲线 / Logistic Growth and S-Curves
`科学 / Scientific`

逻辑斯蒂增长预设，增长面对一个自然的承载容量 K，早期的指数只是一条本就要变平之曲线的左肩，在一个持守之内拟合序列、在当下的约束之下组织规划时有用；它在此到了边界：天花板是进步在其下被观察的那些前提的属性，而不是进步的属性，一条 S 曲线开始变平，暴露的是框架的局限，而当共享的框定松动、持守被重画时，复利就恢复，而相互重叠的 S 曲线，是用尽一条曲线的有限游戏与点燃下一条的取向一起生出的。

Logistic growth presumes that growth faces a natural carrying capacity K and that the early exponential is only the left shoulder of a curve that was always going to flatten, which is of use in fitting series inside a hold and organizing planning under present constraints; it reaches its boundary in that the ceiling is a property of the premises under which progress is observed, not of progress, a flattening S-curve exposes the limits of the frame, compounding resumes when the shared framing loosens and the hold is redrawn, and overlapping S-curves arise from finite games that exhaust one curve together with the orientation that ignites the next.

→ [封闭的假设把复利挤成 S 曲线 / Closed Assumptions Squeeze Compounding into S-Curves](../../posts/closed-assumptions-squeeze-compounding-into-s-curves/) · [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/)

### 无损信息捕获的不可能性 / Lossless Information Capture
`数学 / Mathematical`

拉普拉斯认识论假定能无损记录万物状态以预测未来，脱离语境的精度理想则假定事无巨细的严密准确能担保清晰；在既定框架内检验语法、符号一致性与组件公差时精度有用，但开放的生成性现实超越任何有限编码容量，无损全知在数学上不成立；当事关成败的区分尚未在手头目的所要求的粒度上与附带枝节剥离、或约束变化要求在接触中重切粒度时，局部精确会把上游的含混一路传递下去。

Laplacian epistemology assumes complete state capture allows flawless future prediction, and the ideal of context-free precision assumes exhaustive exactness without remainder guarantees clarity; precision stays useful for checking syntax, notation consistency, and component tolerances within a frame already chosen, but an open-ended generative field outstrips finite encoding, making complete capture impossible, and where consequential distinctions have not been separated from incidental detail at the grain the purpose in hand calls for, or shifting constraints call for re-cutting that grain on contact, local exactness propagates upstream blur.

→ [开放场域中的无损知识不自洽 / Lossless Knowledge of an Open Field Is Incoherent](../../posts/lossless-knowledge-of-an-open-field-is-incoherent/) · [精度与分辨率 / Precision and Resolution](../../posts/precision-and-resolution/)

### 劳动总量固定论 / Lump of Labor
`经济 / Economic`

劳动总量固定论假定经济体中有用工作的总量是一份固定配额，在静态任务清单内核算短期人手编制时有用；它的边界在于常规任务的自动化压低了执行摩擦，并腾出人的容量去应对新浮现的可构想协调问题。

The lump-of-labor thesis presumes a fixed quantum of useful work in an economy, which is of use in accounting for short-term headcount inside a static task list; it reaches its boundary in that automating routine tasks lowers friction and frees human capacity for newly imaginable coordination problems.

→ [当丰裕看起来像稀缺 / When Abundance Looks Like Scarcity](../../posts/when-abundance-looks-like-scarcity/)

### 现代投资组合理论 / Modern Portfolio Theory
`经济 / Economic`

现代投资组合理论以钟形曲线描述回报，在分散配置、控制波动时有用；它在此到了边界：它的钟形与批评它的厚尾一样，都是对已经发生的结果的统计描述，不是可以据以设计策略的生成逻辑。

Modern portfolio theory describes returns with a bell curve, which is of use in diversifying and controlling volatility; it reaches its boundary in that its bell curve, like the heavy tail its critics oppose to it, is a statistical description of outcomes already produced, not the generative logic to design strategy around.

→ [幂律是结果的表象 / Power Law Is the Surface of Outcomes](../../posts/mi-lv-shi-jie-guo-de-biao-xiang/)

### 以买方垄断论证工资下限 / Monopsony Justification for Wage Floors
`经济 / Economic`

以法令设定工资的框架借助市场力量与需求无弹性的假设，在解释局部的买方垄断案例时有用；它在此到了边界：这些假设必须不断重新调校，才能容纳失业与技能积累放慢出现在相对生产率最低者之中的方向性证据。

Frameworks setting wages by decree lean on assumptions about market power and inelastic demand, which is of use in explaining local monopsony cases; it reaches its boundary in that those assumptions need continual retuning to accommodate directional evidence that disemployment and slowed skill accumulation appear among the least productive workers.

→ [财富转移政策与价格下限中的原因错置 / The Misallocation of Cause in Wealth-Transfer Policies and Price Floors](../../posts/the-reversal-of-causality-in-wealth-transfer-policies-and-price-floors/)

### 朴素实在论 / Naive Realism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

朴素实在论假定各观察者共享一幅未经中介、预先造好以待被动检视的场景，作为日常协作的低开销默认起点时有用；它的边界在于现实是在有意识的中心遭遇摩擦、划出新区分的过程中动态实现的，共享现实是各私人投射之间的高度功能共振而非体验的同一，而从一个未加标注的立足点向他人观点贴上“朴素实在论”标签，恰恰重演了它所命名之物。

Naive realism assumes observers share an unmediated, prefabricated scene awaiting passive inspection, which is of use as the low-overhead starting point of everyday coordination; it reaches its boundary in that reality is realized dynamically as conscious centers encounter friction and draw fresh distinctions, shared reality is high functional resonance among private projections rather than identity of experience, and applying 'naive realism' from an unmarked stand to another's view re-enacts what it names.

→ [表征的闭环 / The Closed Loop of Representation](../../posts/the-closed-loop-of-representation/) · [心智最坚固的信念 / The Strongest Belief of the Mind](../../posts/the-strongest-belief-of-the-mind/) · [未经自我审视的批判者 / The Unexamined Critic](../../posts/the-unexamined-critic/) · [现实是心智持续的实现 / Reality Is the Mind's Ongoing Realization](../../posts/reality-is-the-minds-ongoing-realization/)

### 消极功利主义（最小化可避免的苦难） / Negative Utilitarianism (Minimizing Avoidable Suffering)
`哲学 / Philosophical`

最小化可避免的苦难，把苦难当作可以由外部力量减去的对象，作为政策启发时有用；它在此到了边界：它把原因挪离登记苦难的那个中心，于是不断再生它所针对的落差。

Minimizing avoidable suffering treats suffering as an object external agency can subtract, which is of use as a policy heuristic; it reaches its boundary in that it relocates cause away from the center that registers the suffering, and so keeps regenerating the gap it targets.

→ [最初的区分 / The Initial Distinction](../../posts/the-initial-distinction/)

### 神经网络缩放定律 / Neural Scaling Laws
`技术 / Technological`

Kaplan 与 Chinchilla 的缩放定律把既有产物当作待压缩的给定语料，在安排预训练中参数与数据的配比时有用；它们在此到了边界：语料本身在回路中不断扩张，权重只是当前集合的一张有限快照，把它当作上限，就冻结了回路的一个旋钮。

The Kaplan and Chinchilla scaling laws treat existing artifacts as a given corpus to compress, which is of use in allocating parameters and data in pre-training; they reach their boundary in that the corpus itself keeps expanding in the loop, and the weights are a finite snapshot of the current set, so taking them as the limit freezes one dial of the loop.

→ [规模化回路 / The Scaling Loop](../../posts/the-scaling-loop/)

### 没有免费午餐定理 / No Free Lunch Theorem
`数学 / Mathematical`

没有免费午餐定理证明，在一切数学上可能的目标函数之上作均匀平均时，任意两种最优化算法的平均表现严格相等，在揭穿“通用优化器”宣称背后未言明的结构先验时有用；一旦把无结构函数集上的均匀平均投射到具体因果约束使结构先验起决定作用的现实学习场景，该结论便到达了边界。

The No Free Lunch theorems prove that any two optimization algorithms achieve identical average performance when averaged uniformly over all mathematically possible objective functions, which is of use in exposing unstated structural priors behind universal-optimizer claims; the result reaches its boundary when uniform averaging over unstructured function sets is projected onto real-world learning, where concrete causal constraints make structured priors decisive.

→ [被误认作实体的阴影 / The Shadow Mistaken for the Object](../../posts/chollet-intelligence-explosion-analysis/)

### 正态分布 / Normal Distribution
`科学 / Scientific`

正态分布在描述大量离散决策在短窗口里聚集于均值附近时有用；它在此到了边界：它是自由选择事后沉淀出的统计表象，反映的是各心智提取信号能力的不均，对任何一个个体此刻的选择没有因果约束力，被读作不存在真实差异或内在随机，就遮蔽了乘法过程将放大的东西。

The normal distribution is of use in describing how many discrete decisions gather near the mean over a short window; it reaches its boundary in that it is the statistical appearance free choices leave after the fact, reflecting uneven signal-extraction capacity across Minds, with no causal binding force on any individual's choice now, and read as evidence of no real differences or of intrinsic randomness, it hides what multiplication will magnify.

→ [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/) · [目的性的评判与认知的缺省状态 / Judging by Purpose and the Default State of Cognition](../../posts/cong-mu-di-xing-de-ping-pan-kan-ren-zhi-que-sheng/) · [观察的因果僭越与第一人称的收益差 / The Causal Overstep of Observation and the First-Person Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-overstep-of-observation-and-the-first-person-surplus/) · [主权抉择的双面性与观察的客体化 / The Two-Sided Nature of Sovereign Choice and the Objectification of Observation](../../posts/the-two-sided-nature-of-sovereign-choice-and-the-objectification-of-observation/) · [“普通人视角”的解释陷阱 / The Explanatory Trap of the "Ordinary Perspective"](../../posts/the-explanatory-trap-of-the-ordinary-perspective/)

### 观察性行为学 / Observational Behavioral Science
`科学 / Scientific`

将群体在事后沉淀的统计分布逆向设定为微观决策的准入门槛；遗漏了行为选择仅发生在第一人称对净收益与摩擦的具身体感之中，在描述人口学频次与精算规划时保持有效。

Reverses retrospective population distributions into gatekeeping requirements for micro-decisions; it misses that action occurs solely within first-person reckoning of surplus over friction, remaining useful for demographic accounting and actuarial planning.

→ [观察的因果僭越与第一人称的收益差 / The Causal Overstep of Observation and the First-Person Surplus](../../posts/the-causal-overstep-of-observation-and-the-first-person-surplus/)

### OntoAgent 认知架构 / OntoAgent Cognitive Architecture
`技术 / Technological`

符号化的 OntoAgent 认知架构假定自然语言理解可预先登记在显式手工编纂的本体与微理论之中，在可检视的领域解析与可审计的规则执行上有用；一旦无剧本语境超出缺乏自指活态源头的有限静态目录，该架构便到达了边界。

Symbolic OntoAgent architectures assume natural-language understanding can be preregistered in explicit hand-crafted ontologies and microtheories, which is of use for inspectable domain parsing and auditable rule execution; they reach their boundary once unscripted context outstrips finite static catalogs that lack a self-referential living origin.

→ [百科全书式的累加与生成性基底 / Agents in the Long Game of AI](../../posts/agents_leia_ontological_clarity/)

### 协调客观还原理论 / Orchestrated Objective Reduction (Orch-OR)
`科学 / Scientific`

协调客观还原理论假定波函数坍缩为意识提供所需的不可计算性，把测量问题与困难问题合在一起，在为意识寻找物理机制时有用；它在此到了边界：它仍是对单一系统开放性的物理说明。

Orch-OR assumes wavefunction collapse supplies the non-computability consciousness needs, joining the measurement problem and the hard problem, which is of use in seeking a physical mechanism for consciousness; it reaches its boundary in that it remains a physical account of one system's openness.

→ [论封闭系统、开放心智与证明的界限 / On Closed Systems, Open Minds, and the Limits of Proof](../../posts/on-closed-systems-open-minds-and-the-limits-of-proof/) · [另一种相干 / The Other Coherence](../../posts/the-other-coherence/)

### 泛心论 / Panpsychism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

泛心论把主观意识赋予电子等基本粒子；把一切想成以不同速度思考的心智，可以想象，却仍是人的心智按自己所知的样子造出的概念，说电子有意识或没有，都是一个心智把它纳入自己的感知、放下了割点，它在这里到了边界，作为提醒意识不必只在人类大脑皮层里寻找的思路，它依然有用。

Panpsychism attributes conscious awareness to fundamental particles such as electrons; everything as a mind thinking at a different speed can be imagined, yet it is still a concept the human Mind makes after the pattern it knows, and saying the electron is conscious or is not takes it into a Mind's own perception and places the cut, which is where the view reaches its boundary, while as a reminder not to look for consciousness only in the human cortex it stays useful.

→ [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/) · [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/)

### 物理主义 / Physicalism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

物理主义假定第三人称的物质先于并生成心智，在刻画非自反物理相互作用中的不变规律时有用；它遗漏了设定实验边界并读取每一次测量的那位不可还原的第一人称观察者，在此到达了边界。

Physicalism presumes that third-person matter precedes and generates mind, which is of use in modeling invariant regularities across non-reflexive physical interactions; it reaches its boundary in omitting the irreducible first-person observer who sets experimental bounds and reads every measurement.

→ [因果的自反性与物理的无源假定 / Causality is Irreducible; The Physical is a View from Nowhere](../../posts/causality-is-irreducible-the-physical-is-a-view-from-nowhere/)

### 普朗克尺度 / Planck Scale
`科学 / Scientific`

普朗克时间与普朗克长度标出现有模型的分辨力用尽的尺度，c = ℓ_P / t_P 把它们与光速连在一起，在计算中有用；把它们读成刻在宇宙容器上、先于观察的最短时长与最短距离，在此到了边界，它们描述的是观察者自己由因果步长织成的感知分辨率。

The Planck time and Planck length mark the scale where present models run out of resolution, and c = ℓ_P / t_P ties them to the speed of light, of use in calculation; reading them as a shortest duration and distance engraved on a cosmic container prior to observation is where they reach their boundary, since they describe the perceptual resolution of an observer whose own apparatus is woven from causal steps.

→ [时间是因果的不可逆方向与离散步长 / Time Is the Irreversible Direction and Discrete Step of Causality](../../posts/time-is-causality-not-a-dimension/) · [黑白采样帧与外推宇宙 / The Black-and-White Sample Frame and the Extrapolated Universe](../../posts/the-black-and-white-sample-frame-and-the-extrapolated-universe/)

### 幂律分布 / Power Law Distribution
`科学 / Scientific`

幂律在描述某个观测窗口里结果的形状时有用；它在此到了边界：它被升格为必须服从的规律时，聚合的痕迹被误认作活动本身，而生成它的是复利的速率差。

The power law is of use in describing the shape of outcomes within an observation window; it reaches its boundary in that, once raised into a law that must be obeyed, aggregated traces are taken for the activity itself, while what generates them is the differential of compounding rates.

→ [幂律是结果的表象 / Power Law Is the Surface of Outcomes](../../posts/mi-lv-shi-jie-guo-de-biao-xiang/) · [幂律是正态分布的长期后果 / Power Law Is the Long-Term Consequence of Normal Distribution](../../posts/power-law-is-the-long-term-consequence-of-normal-distribution/) · [可延展的地平线 / The Extendable Horizon](../../posts/the-extendable-horizon/) · [现实扭曲力场颠倒了基线 / The Reality Distortion Field Inverts the Baseline](../../posts/the-reality-distortion-field-inverts-the-baseline/) · [让渡主权的幂律法则 / The Power-Law of Surrendered Agency](../../posts/the-power-law-of-surrendered-agency/) · [主权抉择的双面性与观察的客体化 / The Two-Sided Nature of Sovereign Choice and the Objectification of Observation](../../posts/the-two-sided-nature-of-sovereign-choice-and-the-objectification-of-observation/)

### 他心问题 / Problem of Other Minds
`哲学 / Philosophical`

他心问题假定将意识归属于自身以外者必须依赖源自外部观察的演绎证明，否则便会滑入怀疑论；这一框架在区分无需验证的第一人称直接登记与跨心智归属时有用，而其边界在于，对其他心智的归属并非单靠演绎证明或行为输出，而是建立在植根于共有生物、演化与代谢相似性的类比推断之上。

The problem of other minds presumes that attributing consciousness beyond one's own case requires deductive proof from external observation or else collapses into skepticism; this framing is useful for separating immediate first-person registration, which needs no verification, from cross-mind attribution, and reaches its boundary where attribution to other minds rests not on deductive proof or behavioral output alone but on analogical inference grounded in shared biological, evolutionary, and metabolic similarity.

→ [NPC 印象是结构性的，而非心智的排名 / The NPC Impression Is Structural, Not a Ranking of Minds](../../posts/the-npc-impression-is-structural/) · [折叠的连续谱 / The Continuum of the Fold](../../posts/the-continuum-of-the-fold/) · [表征的闭环 / The Closed Loop of Representation](../../posts/the-closed-loop-of-representation/) · [归属的不对称 / Asymmetry of Ascription](../../posts/asymmetry-of-ascription/) · [意识的魔术戏法与推断的非对称性 / The Sleight of Hand of Consciousness and the Asymmetry of Inference](../../posts/the-sleight-of-hand-of-consciousness-and-the-asymmetry-of-inference/)

### 生产过剩与意识形态消费 / Productive Surplus and Ideology
`经济 / Economic`

后稀缺文化批判假定不断充裕的物质剩余将道德与意识形态评判从物质约束中解放出来、从而可居高临下地审视生产，在登记丰裕如何拓宽非生存带宽时有用；一旦由持续生产剩余资助并放大的价值取向把这份剩余当成自动维系的背景，反过来围剿支撑自身的风险承担型生产，便到达了边界。

Post-scarcity cultural critique presumes that rising material surplus liberates moral and ideological evaluation from physical constraints so production can be judged from above, which is of use in registering how abundance widens non-survival bandwidth; it reaches its boundary when orientations funded and amplified by ongoing productive surplus treat that surplus as self-maintaining background and campaign against the risk-bearing production that sustains them.

→ [生产创造自己的意识形态消费 / Production Creates Its Own Ideological Consumption](../../posts/fate-loves-irony-refined/)

### 理性选择理论 / Rational Choice Theory
`经济 / Economic`

理性选择理论预设偏好结构足够一致、足够共享，可以为加总、均衡与政策处方奠基，在对已发生的选择作事后重构时有用；它在此到了边界：私人且变动的前提给不出这种一致与稳定，于是它解释过去很强，预见未来很弱。

Rational choice theory presumes preference structures consistent and shared enough to ground aggregation, equilibrium and policy prescription, which is of use in reconstructing choices already made; it reaches its boundary in that private, shifting premises cannot supply that consistency and stability, so it fits the past well and foresees poorly.

→ [理性选择公设是原罪 / The Rational Choice Postulate Is the Original Sin](../../posts/the-rational-choice-postulate-is-the-original-sin/)

### 强化学习 / Reinforcement Learning (RL)
`技术 / Technological`

强化学习通过状态-动作轨迹上的标量奖励最大化来形式化策略最优化，在有界博弈与规则明确的环境中训练控制策略时有用；一旦被投射为自主能动性的模型便到达了边界，因为计算策略优化的是固定的代理标量，而活的心智保有追问并改写何为奖励的主权。

Reinforcement learning formalizes policy optimization through scalar reward maximization over state-action trajectories, which is of use for training control policies inside bounded games and well-specified environments; it reaches its boundary when projected as a model of autonomous agency, since computational policies optimize fixed proxy scalars whereas living Minds hold sovereignty to question and redefine what counts as reward.

→ [看似AI的强化学习，实为人类的自我强化学习 / What Looks Like RL for AIs Is Self-RL for Humans](../../posts/self-rl-for-humans/)

### 时间可逆性悖论 / Reversibility Paradox of Time
`科学 / Scientific`

时间可逆性悖论假定时间对称的微观方程使宏观不可逆性显得反常，在厘清单纯哈密顿动力学未加规定之处时有用；该悖论忘记了对称方程本是从不可逆的第一人称观测与记录保存内部抽出的无时间形式压缩，在此到达了边界。

The reversibility paradox of time presumes that time-symmetric microscopic equations make macroscopic irreversibility anomalous, which is of use in clarifying what Hamiltonian dynamics alone leaves unspecified; the paradox reaches its boundary in forgetting that symmetric equations are timeless formal compressions drawn from within irreversible first-person observation and record-keeping.

→ [时间之箭的伪悖论与对称模拟的边界 / The Mirage of Time's Arrow and the Boundaries of Symmetric Simulation](../../posts/the-mirage-of-times-arrow-and-the-boundaries-of-simulation/) · [时间是因果的不可逆方向与离散步长 / Time Is the Irreversible Direction and Discrete Step of Causality](../../posts/time-is-causality-not-a-dimension/)

### 黎曼猜想 / Riemann Hypothesis
`数学 / Mathematical`

黎曼猜想将 ζ(s) 解析延拓的所有非平凡零点置于临界线 Re(s) = 1/2 上——为围绕平滑对数渐近线的素数分布涨落给出上界，同时有限阶段比值 N₀(T)/N(T) 得以认证诸如 41.6% 至 67.2% 的临时比例下界——在解析数论与素数渐近分析中颇具效用；一旦解析延拓或阶段比值极限被读作对无限整体的已完成清点，或被当作从形式对称性推导物理不确定性的存在论桥梁，便到达了边界。

The Riemann Hypothesis places all nontrivial zeros of the analytic continuation of ζ(s) on the critical line Re(s) = 1/2—bounding prime-distribution fluctuations around smooth logarithmic asymptotics while finite stage-ratios N₀(T)/N(T) certify provisional proportion bounds such as 41.6 to 67.2 percent—which is of use in analytic number theory and prime asymptotics; it reaches its boundary when analytic continuation or stage-ratio limits are read as a completed survey over an infinite totality or as an ontological bridge deriving physical indeterminacy from formal symmetry.

→ [潜无限与数学思维的暂时闭合 / Potential Infinity and the Temporary Closures of Mathematical Thought](../../posts/potential-infinity-and-the-temporary-closures-of-mathematical-thought/) · [全息回声 / The Holographic Echo](../../posts/the-holographic-echo-reverse-engineering-quantum-indeterminacy-via-the-riemann-zeta-function/)

### 施密特敌友之分 / Schmittian Friend-Enemy Distinction
`意识形态 / Ideological`

施密特把敌友之分立为政治的判准，可用来指认公共动员何时已把本可分开的宗教、经济或文化分歧聚拢为同一组联合与分离；它的边界在于，一旦把这一判准奉为政治不可化约的基底，具体争端还未按各自的坐标轴受审视，各条独立坐标轴就已被压缩到单一的敌意轴上。

Carl Schmitt posits the friend-enemy distinction as the criterion of the political, which is of use in registering when public mobilization has gathered separable religious, economic, or cultural disagreements into one grouping of association and dissociation; it reaches its boundary in that holding this criterion as the irreducible ground of politics compresses independent axes onto a single hostility axis before concrete disputes are examined on their own axes.

→ [安装战争的那个问题 / The Question That Installs the War](../../posts/the-question-that-installs-the-war/)

### 科学实在论 / Scientific Realism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

实在论假定独立于心智的实在先于并产生心智；对这一底层的任何描述都已是理解的行动，无奇迹论证所援引的预测成功只在模型之间挑选、不给起点增添效力，因此这条箭头只是一个回路有用的一面。

Realism assumes mind-independent reality precedes and produces the mind; every description of that substrate is already an act of understanding, and the predictive success the no-miracles argument appeals to selects among models without adding validity to the starting point, so the arrow is one useful face of a loop.

→ [因果与不可化约的先在 / Causality and the Irreducible Prior](../../posts/causality-and-the-irreducible-prior/)

### 后发优势 / Second-Mover (Fast Follower) Advantage
`经济 / Economic`

后发优势的叙事说耐心的跟随者等市场明朗再入场收割，在描述一些后来者确实借先驱的试错降低了成本时有用；它在此到了边界：它以公开发布的日期去读历史，看不到胜者早在方向仍被共识打发时就押注了更高杠杆的架构，那份未经验证的复利只是延后显形，而这种读法本身也是从结果倒着读回去的。

The second-mover story says the patient follower waits for the market to clear and then collects the spoils, which is of use in describing how some latecomers do lower their costs on the pioneers' trial and error; it reaches its boundary in that it reads history by launch dates and misses that the winners committed to a higher-leverage architecture while consensus still dismissed the direction, so the unvalidated compounding only surfaces late, a reading that is itself taken backward from the outcome.

→ [看不见的第一步 / The Invisible First Move](../../posts/the-invisible-first-move/)

### 香农信息论 / Shannon Information Theory
`数学 / Mathematical`

信道容量计数的是相对于约定划分已经完成的二元区分，在通信工程上严谨有用；它在此到了边界：它按设计排除了意义，并不回答信息是什么，把这把尺当成信息本身，度量的动作就脱落了。

Channel capacity counts already-performed binary distinctions relative to an agreed partition, rigorous and of use in communication engineering; it reaches its boundary in that it excludes meaning by design and does not say what information is, and taking this ruler for information itself lets the act of measuring drop out.

→ [信息是什么 / What Information Is](../../posts/what-information-is/) · [向量与投影的迷局 / The Vector and the Puzzle of Projections](../../posts/the-vector-and-the-puzzle-of-projections/)

### 模拟宇宙假说 / Simulation Hypothesis
`哲学 / Philosophical`

模拟宇宙假说由形式与计算同构外推，提出实在是在外部宿主机上执行的软件程序，作为探查物理规律之信息刻画的思想实验时富有启发；它将符号同构误认作存在论同一，并在发问者未经审视的前提之内搭起一条由模拟者与基底套叠而成的封闭神学走廊，在此到达了边界。

The simulation hypothesis extrapolates from formal and computational isomorphism to propose that reality is software executing on an external host computer, which is of use as a thought experiment probing informational descriptions of physical regularities; it reaches its boundary in mistaking symbolic isomorphism for ontological identity and building a closed theological corridor of simulators and base substrates inside the questioner's own unexamined premise.

→ [凡可言说皆不可言说之产物 / All That Can Be Spoken Is the Product of the Unspeakable](../../posts/all-that-can-be-spoken-is-the-product-of-the-unspeakable/) · [宏观现象的生成图谱 / The Generative Atlas of Macroscopic Phenomena](../../posts/the-generative-atlas-of-macroscopic-phenomena/) · [自由变量的同构与先验主权 / The Isomorphism of Free Variables and Prior Sovereignty](../../posts/zi-you-bian-liang-de-tong-gou-yu-xian-yan-zhu-quan/) · [智能最容易困住的正是它自己 / Intelligence Is the Thing Most Likely to Trap Itself](../../posts/intelligence-traps-itself/)

### 热力学第二定律 / Second Law of Thermodynamics
`科学 / Scientific`

热力学第二定律与“过去假说”将宏观时间非对称性建模为时间可逆微观定律之上自所设低熵初始边界条件出发的统计熵增，在热机效率、统计力学与宇宙学建模中行之有效；一旦要求靠一套对称的相空间记账规则外加事后拟合的初始边界，去反推每一次测量与记忆记录早已预设的不可逆步进因果方向，便到达了边界。

The Second Law of Thermodynamics and the Past Hypothesis model macroscopic time asymmetry as statistical entropy growth from a posited low-entropy initial boundary condition over time-reversible micro-laws, which is of use in heat engines, statistical mechanics, and cosmological modeling; they reach their boundary when a symmetric phase-space bookkeeping rule plus a retrospectively fitted initial boundary is asked to derive the irreversible step-by-step direction of causality that every measurement and memory record already presupposes.

→ [时间之箭的伪悖论与对称模拟的边界 / The Mirage of Time's Arrow and the Boundaries of Symmetric Simulation](../../posts/the-mirage-of-times-arrow-and-the-boundaries-of-simulation/) · [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/)

### 社会契约论 / Social Contract Theory
`哲学 / Philosophical`

社会契约论把社会呈现为一种需要被正当化的约束，在追问制度凭什么约束人时有用；它在此到了边界：主权由每一个行动着的心智依本性持有，而非由承认、选举或立约之时的让渡所授予，社会契约描述的是个人为协调与承诺而自由做出的选择的聚合痕迹，这份痕迹成为后来的自由选择所依托的背景，而不是从外部压下的枷锁，不同惯例之间变化的只是功劳、归责与下一步判断在行动边缘被登记的宽度。

Social contract theory presents society as a constraint that must be justified, which is of use in asking by what right institutions bind people; it reaches its boundary in that sovereignty is held by nature at every acting Mind rather than conferred by recognition, election, or founding transfer, and the social contract describes the aggregated trace of choices freely made to coordinate and commit—a trace that becomes the background for later free choices rather than chains pressed on from outside, while what varies across conventions is only how widely credit, blame, and next-step judgment are registered at the acting edge.

→ [自由即地基 / Freedom as Ground](../../posts/freedom-as-ground/) · [行使的残余与政治的稳定 / The Residue of Exercise and the Stabilization of Politics](../../posts/the-residue-of-exercise-and-the-stabilization-of-politics/) · [良知、委托与管辖权错位 / Conscience, Delegation, and the Jurisdiction Error](../../posts/conscience-power-delegation/)

### 狭义相对论 / Special Relativity
`科学 / Scientific`

狭义相对论确立了空间与时间间隔随惯性参照系变化而光速保持不变，在跨运动观察者调和电动力学与力学时行之有效；一旦在消解表观佯谬时暗中偷渡回一位无锚定的全局观察者，或把不变的渲染节律误认作静态时空容器，便到达了边界。

Special relativity establishes that spatial and temporal intervals vary across inertial frames while keeping the speed of light invariant, which is of use in reconciling electrodynamics and mechanics across moving observers; it reaches its boundary when resolving apparent paradoxes sneaks an unanchored global observer back into the frame or mistakes invariant rendering cadence for a static spacetime container.

→ [双生子佯谬与隐秘观察者 / The Twin Paradox and the Hidden Observer](../../posts/the-twin-paradox-and-the-hidden-observer/) · [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/)

### 自发物质涌现论 / Spontaneous Material Emergence
`科学 / Scientific`

自发物质涌现论以基底内部足够复杂的相互作用、非线性与不连续性会自行生成新能力与宏观定律为前提，这在选定持守之下组织密集的相互作用、跨尺度追踪不易预料的能力时有用；它在此到了边界：把复杂性当作生成之源，便使搭起这一持守的观察心智之前提择取与分辨率选择，连同在先前持守用尽余地之处被登记的开放继续，一并从叙述中脱落了。

Spontaneous material emergence takes as its premise that sufficiently complex interactions, nonlinearities, and discontinuities within a substrate generate novel capabilities and macro-level laws on their own, which is of use in organizing dense interactions under a chosen hold and tracking unanticipated capabilities across scales; it reaches its boundary in that treating complexity as the generative source drops out both the observing Mind's premise-selection and choice of resolution that erect the hold and the open continuation that registers where the prior hold runs out of room.

→ [复杂性遮蔽了涌现之为心智的行动 / Complexity Obscures Emergence as the Act of Mind](../../posts/complexity-obscures-emergence-as-the-act-of-mind/) · [涌现的几何学 / The Geometry of Emergence](../../posts/the-geometry-of-emergence/)

### 斯多葛主义 / Stoicism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

斯多葛主义预设属于自己的主导理性只有在逻各斯指派的宇宙角色中才得到恰当的表达，在训练内在的平静时有用；它在此到了边界：它把被肯定为自主的心智重新放进一个比它更大的理性秩序。

Stoicism presumes the ruling faculty that is one's own finds proper expression only in a cosmic role assigned by the Logos, which is of use in training inner equanimity; it reaches its boundary in that it reinserts the mind it affirms as autonomous into a rational order larger than itself.

→ [自由的心智被声称保护它的东西取代 / The Free Mind Is Displaced by What Claims to Protect It](../../posts/the-free-mind-is-displaced-by-what-claims-to-protect-it/) · [心智的几何学 / The Geometry of Mind](../../posts/the-geometry-of-mind/)

### 主观价值理论 / Subjective Theory of Value
`经济 / Economic`

主观价值理论把价值放在活的心智的评价里，这一点有用，第一人称的价值没有外部的尺度；它在此到了边界：价值一旦被记成可储存的数量，借据、货币与资产就被误认为财富本身，而它们只是时间延迟留下的滞后记录，财富在个人实时的创造能力里。

The subjective theory of value places value in the valuations of living minds, which is of use, since first-person value has no outside scale; it reaches its boundary once value is recorded as a storable quantity and IOUs, money and assets are taken for wealth itself, when they are only lagging records left by temporal delay and wealth lies in people's real-time capability to create.

→ [价值、货币与财富的生成机制 / The Generative Mechanics of Value, Money, and Wealth](../../posts/the-generative-mechanics-of-value-money-and-wealth/)

### 技术封建主义 / Techno-Feudalism
`经济 / Economic`

技术封建论认为资本买下算力就会把人的等级永久冻结，“普通人”跃迁的窗口正在关上，作为对资本集中的观察有用；它在此到了边界：长期位置由复利的改进速率决定，有限的起点会被速率的持续差异压过，AI 只放大带给它的回路，把原因放到外部结构上的信念会自我实现。

Techno-feudalism holds that capital buying compute will freeze human hierarchy for good and that the window for "ordinary people" to leap is closing, of use as an observation of capital concentration; it reaches its boundary in that long-run standing is decided by the compounded rate of improvement, a finite start is overwhelmed by a persistent rate difference, AI only amplifies the loop brought to it, and the belief that places cause in outside structures fulfils itself.

→ [能动性、复利与 AI 封建之争 / Agency, Compounding, and the Argument Over AI Feudalism](../../posts/agency-compounding-and-the-argument-over-ai-feudalism/)

### 技术决定论 / Technological Determinism
`技术 / Technological`

技术决定论预设技术是一股立于人类之外、推动或支配人类的力量，在追踪工具如何改变可做之事时有用；它在此到了边界：技术是人类被自身相继的修改因子连乘之后超出基线的残差，没有独立的基底，也不能逆转塑造的方向。

Technological determinism presumes technology is a force standing apart from humanity that drives or governs it, which is of use in tracking how tools change what can be done; it reaches its boundary in that technology is the excess of Human, multiplied by successive factors of its own modification, over the baseline, with no separate substrate and no power to reverse the direction of shaping.

→ [技术是人类复利的残差 / Technology Is Residual of Human Compounding](../../posts/technology-is-residual-of-human-compounding/)

### 技术奇点理论 / Technological Singularity
`技术 / Technological`

技术奇点理论预设，复利增长的能力曲线会越过一道门槛，过了它，器物便越过自己的源头去改进自身，在登记前沿上亲身感到的加速、在为努力动员资源时有用；它在此到了边界：门槛永远立在画出它的边缘前方，把一条局部的曲线外推，是把图像保留到它那一步之后，而更密的脚手架仍是由心智掌舵的脚手架。

Technological Singularity theory presumes that a compounding capability curve crosses a threshold past which the artifact improves itself beyond its source, which is of use in registering the acceleration felt at the frontier and in mobilizing effort; it reaches its boundary in that the threshold sits forever ahead of the edge that draws it, extrapolating a local curve keeps the image past its step, and denser scaffolding stays scaffolding steered by the Mind.

→ [指数级进程与线性之眼 / The Exponential and the Linear Eye](../../posts/Analysis_Adolescence_of_Technology/) · [自我放大的制品 / The Artifacts of Self-Amplification](../../posts/the-artifacts-of-self-amplification/) · [超越的定相 / The Fixed Image of Transcendence](../../posts/the-fixed-image-of-transcendence/) · [AGI 与 ASI 是加速通用智能的暂时球门 / AGI and ASI Are Temporary Goalposts](../../posts/agi-and-asi-are-temporary-goalposts/) · [牛肝菌、算力风暴与普罗米修斯的火 / Boletus, the Compute Storm, and Promethean Fire](../../posts/boletus-compute-storm-and-promethean-fire/) · [数字神祇与视界跳跃 / The Digital God and the Horizon Jump](../../posts/the-digital-god-and-the-horizon-jump/)

### 技术性失业 / Technological Unemployment
`经济 / Economic`

技术毁灭岗位还是创造岗位，预设由单一的总量来裁决，在追踪某一行业的岗位变化时有用；它在此到了边界：前沿以净增量创造新的领域，创造几乎是给定的，旧工作的淘汰是新工作份额扩大的后果，并在几十年的互补资本投入里展开，而不是一对一的替换。

Whether technology destroys or creates jobs presumes that one aggregate decides, which is of use in tracking the jobs of a particular sector; it reaches its boundary in that the frontier creates new domains as net addition, creation is near given, and the obsolescence of old work follows as new work's share grows, unfolding over decades of complementary capital rather than as one-for-one replacement.

→ [AI 争论中的观察划界 / The Observational Cut in AI Debates](../../posts/the-observational-cut-in-ai-debates/) · [技术变革的局部陷阱 / The Local Trap of Technological Change](../../posts/the-local-trap-of-technological-change/)

### 意识的困难问题 / The Hard Problem of Consciousness
`哲学 / Philosophical`

困难问题预设主观体验与物理过程之间有一道需要被解释、也就可能被合上的鸿沟，在标出第三人称描述留下的剩余时有用；它在此到了边界：这份难，是闭合的要求加在剩余的开放上造出来的，而剩余的开放正是学习得以继续的地方，既不待删除，也不待最终合上。

The hard problem presumes a gap between subjective experience and physical process that calls for explanation and so might be closed, which is of use in marking the residual that third-person description leaves; it reaches its boundary in that the hardness is made by the demand for closure laid on residual openness, and that openness is where learning continues, neither awaiting deletion nor scheduled for final closure.

→ [意识的难题与学习相容 / The Hard Problem of Consciousness Is Consistent with Learning](../../posts/the-hard-problem-of-consciousness-is-consistent-with-learning/) · [意识的魔术戏法与推断的非对称性 / The Sleight of Hand of Consciousness and the Asymmetry of Inference](../../posts/the-sleight-of-hand-of-consciousness-and-the-asymmetry-of-inference/)

### 万物理论 / Theory of Everything (ToE)
`科学 / Scientific`

万物理论寻求用单一封闭方程解释一切物理现象，在跨尺度统一局部相互作用对称性与简化预测计算时有用；它要求一份静态形式目录将自身的观察者与时间生成一并收容在内，而因果律要求在先前因与相异后果之间保有一道不可闭合的非对称，在此到达了边界。

Theories of Everything seek a single closed equation explaining all physical phenomena, which is of use in unifying local interaction symmetries and simplifying predictive calculation across regimes; they reach their boundary in demanding that a static formal catalog contain its own observer and temporal generation, whereas causality requires an irreducible asymmetry between prior cause and distinct effect.

→ [非万物之理 / Not a Theory of Everything](../../posts/not-a-theory-of-everything/) · [非万物之理·重溯 / Not a ToE Retraced](../../posts/not-a-toe-rewrite/) · [索求万物理论是在将当下冻结为清单 / Demanding a Theory of Everything Freezes Time into a Catalog](../../posts/demanding-a-toe-freezes-time-into-a-catalog/) · [当电子开始思考 / When Electrons Think](../../posts/when-electrons-think/) · [令人彻夜难眠的四个追问与投射的边界 / Four Questions That Keep You Up at Night](../../posts/four-night-questions/)

### 功利主义抽象道德账本 / Utilitarian Moral Ledger
`哲学 / Philosophical`

功利主义道德核算通过在单一标量账本上无偏汇总一切有情众生的福祉来评估行动，在超越狭隘利益审视宏观公共政策权衡时有用；一旦抽离的全局算术压过具身的局部义务，把具体处境中的道德责任当成可互换的数值余额，便到达了边界。

Utilitarian moral accounting evaluates action by aggregating impartial welfare across all sentient beings on a single scalar ledger, which is of use in auditing broad public-policy trade-offs beyond parochial interest; it reaches its boundary when detached global arithmetic overrides embodied local obligations and treats situated moral responsibility as an interchangeable numerical balance.

→ [未被遭遇之物的账本 / The Ledger of the Unencountered](../../posts/the-ledger-of-the-unencountered/)

### 乌托邦社会工程 / Utopian Social Engineering
`意识形态 / Ideological`

乌托邦社会工程预设，一个具体而融贯的善的理想可以立为所有人的标准，并由一份确定的秩序来实现，在一个心智私下珍视一种更好的构型、并把它作为邀请或范例供人自由拾取时有用；它在此到了边界：心智在目的与解释上非同一，这个元前提在挑战之下重申自己，所以理想一旦被抬升为每个心智须接受的尺度，便把每一个目的不同者的偏好目的计为缺陷，连共同的口号也留下余下的读法，强制把它们重新归类为不服从。

Utopian social engineering presumes that a concrete and coherent ideal of the good can be installed as the standard for all and realized as a determinate order, which is of use where a mind privately cherishes a better configuration and offers it as invitation or model for free uptake; it reaches its boundary in that minds are non-identical in ends and interpretation, a meta-premise that reaffirms itself under challenge, so the ideal, once elevated into the measure every Mind must accept, scores the preferred ends of every mind that differs as defects, and even shared slogans leave residual readings that enforcement reclassifies as non-compliance.

→ [对乌托邦的信念是通往反乌托邦之路 / The Belief in Utopia Is the Path to Dystopia](../../posts/the-belief-in-utopia-is-the-path-to-dystopia/)

### 福利国家依赖困境 / Welfare State Dependency
`经济 / Economic`

福利国家假定高税收是互助社会必要而高尚的代价，国家能兜底地生成福祉，给受助者即时缓解时有用；它在此到了边界：集体不是心智，不生成价值，公共成本被摊薄到感觉不到、所纳之税被放大成牺牲，长期资助超出产出的消费会关掉个人的损失函数，压抑没能生成的财富。

The welfare state assumes high taxation is the necessary, noble price of a supportive society and that the state can generate well-being as a backstop, of use in giving recipients immediate relief; it reaches its boundary in that a collective is not a Mind and generates no value, public cost is diffused until weightless while the tax paid is amplified into sacrifice, and funding consumption beyond production over the long run switches off the personal loss function and suppresses wealth never generated.

→ [福利国家的幻象 / The Welfare State Illusion](../../posts/the-welfare-state-illusion-cost-diffusion-and-agency-atrophy/)

### 辉格史观（必然进步论） / Whig Theory of History
`意识形态 / Ideological`

辉格史观预设历史沿一条通往进步的必然弧线展开，繁荣是这条弧线自己的产物，在回看时把一连串改进排成一条线索时有用；它在此到了边界：进步是一串由稀少的少数发起的离散行动，繁荣的指标与共同的情绪都是对这些行动的延迟登记，发起的所在一旦变稀，后来可登记的就更少，没有哪条弧线替它们发起。

The Whig theory of history presumes that history unfolds along an inevitable arc toward progress, with prosperity as that arc's own product, which is of use in lining up a run of improvements in retrospect; it reaches its boundary in that progress is a run of discrete acts initiated by thin minorities, prosperity figures and shared mood are delayed registrations of those acts, and when the initiating loci thin there is less to register later, since no arc initiates in their place.

→ [进步叙事中的症候与原因 / Symptom and Cause in the Narratives of Progress](../../posts/symptom-and-cause-in-the-narratives-of-progress/)

### 米田引理的对偶结构 / Yoneda Lemma Duality
`数学 / Mathematical`

米田引理借范畴里与对象相连的全部态射网络（包括对象自身的恒等态射）来刻画对象，在范畴论中深具威力；当结构主义的读法把这张网当作全然外在，并略去发起态射、划下切分的第一人称原点时，它就到了边界。

The Yoneda lemma characterizes an object through the full web of morphisms linking it across a category—including its own identity morphism—which is powerful across category theory; it reaches its boundary when a structuralist reading treats that web as purely external and drops the first-person origin that initiates morphisms and draws the cut.

→ [共存的几何学 / The Geometry of Coexistence](../../posts/the-geometry-of-coexistence-and-the-dual-cut/)

### 芝诺悖论 / Zeno's Paradoxes
`数学 / Mathematical`

用收敛级数化解阿基里斯追龟，预设连续运动与固定赛道，在这一抽象框架内把无穷级数当作有限时间内可走完时有用；它在此到了边界：前沿的赛道被领先者不断延长，这一解法不能直接移用。

The convergent-series resolution of Achilles and the tortoise presumes continuous motion on a static course, which is of use in treating an infinite series as completable in finite time inside that abstracted frame; it reaches its boundary in that a frontier's course is lengthened by its leaders, so the resolution does not transfer directly.

→ [抽象、边界与现实的移动边缘 / Abstraction Boundaries and the Moving Edge of Reality](../../posts/abstraction-boundaries-and-the-moving-edge-of-reality/)

### 玻姆力学 / Bohmian Mechanics
`科学 / Scientific`

玻姆力学在位形空间上引入非定域导向场与连续粒子轨迹以在形式上恢复量子领域的决定论，作为表明标准量子预测并不唯一强制正统波包坍缩图景的自洽数学对照方案时有用；它在经验预测上与标准量子力学不可分辨，只是把观察者的测量视界移置进了不可观测的初始粒子构型之中，在此到达了边界。

Bohmian mechanics introduces a nonlocal guiding field and continuous particle trajectories across configuration space to restore formal determinism to the quantum domain, which is of use as a consistent mathematical counterpoint showing that standard quantum predictions do not uniquely force orthodox wave-collapse imagery; it reaches its boundary in remaining empirically indistinguishable from standard quantum mechanics while relocating the observer's measurement horizon into unobservable initial particle configurations.

→ [算符的潜无限与记录的有限边界 / The Potential Infinity of Operators and the Finite Horizon of Records](../../posts/the-potential-infinity-of-operators-and-the-finite-horizon-of-records/)

### 多世界诠释 / Many-Worlds Interpretation
`哲学 / Philosophical`

多世界诠释假定薛定谔方程的线性幺正演化作为穷尽的物理实在成立、一切未观测的互斥分支皆并存不悖，在形式量子计算内部免除外加坍缩公设时有用；它用观察者身处哪一分支的指示性事实替换宏观记录的唯一性，却未能从先前局部事实推导出特定结果或不可逆步进的显现，在此到达了边界。

The Many-Worlds Interpretation presumes that the linear unitary evolution of the Schrödinger equation holds as an exhaustive physical reality where every unobserved mutually exclusive branch co-exists, which is of use for eliminating ad hoc collapse postulates inside formal quantum calculation; it reaches its boundary in replacing the uniqueness of macroscopic records with an indexical fact of observer location without deriving the appearance of a particular outcome or irreversible step from prior local facts.

→ [算符的潜无限与记录的有限边界 / The Potential Infinity of Operators and the Finite Horizon of Records](../../posts/the-potential-infinity-of-operators-and-the-finite-horizon-of-records/) · [时间之箭的伪悖论与对称模拟的边界 / The Mirage of Time's Arrow and the Boundaries of Symmetric Simulation](../../posts/the-mirage-of-times-arrow-and-the-boundaries-of-simulation/) · [令人彻夜难眠的四个追问与投射的边界 / Four Questions That Keep You Up at Night](../../posts/four-night-questions/)

### 超决定论 / Superdeterminism
`科学 / Scientific`

超决定论设定宇宙太初初始条件预先将隐变量与未来每一位实验者的探测器设定关联起来，使贝尔关联在形式上同时保留定域性与决定论，在标出保留定域隐变量所需走到的逻辑极端时有用；它消解了经验科学自身赖以成立的实验探究统计独立性，在此到达了边界。

Superdeterminism posits that primordial initial conditions pre-correlate hidden variables with every future experimenter's detector settings so Bell correlations preserve formal locality and determinism, which is of use in mapping the logical extreme required to retain local hidden variables; it reaches its boundary by undermining the statistical independence of experimental inquiry on which empirical science itself relies.

→ [算符的潜无限与记录的有限边界 / The Potential Infinity of Operators and the Finite Horizon of Records](../../posts/the-potential-infinity-of-operators-and-the-finite-horizon-of-records/)

### 技术先锋主义 / Technological Vanguardism
`意识形态 / Ideological`

技术先锋主义假定一旦观察者登记到某项局部突破，外部制度与同侪便须即刻与其必然性同步，在早期孤身构筑阶段维系信念时有用；它忘记了非共识优势恰恰仰赖时间上的认知时滞，要求即刻达成共识反而会抹平前沿落差本身，在此到达了边界。

Technological vanguardism presumes that once an observer registers a localized breakthrough, external institutions and peers must immediately synchronize with its necessity, which is of use in sustaining conviction during early solitary building; it reaches its boundary in forgetting that non-consensus advantage depends on temporal recognition lag and that demanding instant consensus would erase the frontier differential itself.

→ [独见者的自反困境与必然性的后验投射 / The Reflexive Dilemma of Unique Insight and the Retrospective Projection of Necessity](../../posts/the-reflexive-dilemma-of-unique-insight/)

### 相容论 / Compatibilism
`哲学 / Philosophical`

相容论将自由意志重新界定为即便内在欲求已由先在条件与物理定律悉数固定、仍与行动者内在欲求相契合的行动，在法律与日常语境中区分自愿行为与外在胁迫时依然好用；当“被固定与否”从外部把抉择当成一件已完成的对象来追问，而非视作因果决定本身借以被登记的那道鲜活第一人称切分时，这一前提便到达了边界。
